1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any dna module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 680. The projective resolution has length at most 638 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 680 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the dna structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 638. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 219.3 (Optimality Condition)
